# QA coverage: screen x configuration

Generated by `scripts/qa_coverage.py` from `qa/coverage/` (catalog, plan, what the harness produced, the contact sheets, and which sheets were looked at).
A combination counts as verified only if it was **produced** and **viewed** at a readable size (a contact sheet of the screen under several configurations; 
a zoomed crop where the text is small). Anything not viewed is `UNVERIFIED` and is never counted as a pass. `QA_LEDGER.md` has the findings.

## How a combination is produced, and what "viewed" means

- **Screens.** `ScreenCatalog` (`Packages/NoctisKit/QA/QACatalog`) lists every screen, sheet and state the Debug launch arguments can reach (193 iPhone entries
  plus 19 for the Apple Watch). A unit test (`ScreenCatalogTests`) fails on a duplicate, on an `AppRoute` case no entry opens, on a route that does not
  parse, on a Settings page, Recovery range or factor with no entry and on a launch flag no source reads. `qa/coverage/catalog.json` is its export.
- **Configurations.** `scripts/qa_configs.py` names each one; `scripts/qa_matrix.py` puts the simulator in it (`simctl ui` for appearance, content size and
  Increase Contrast; the simulated device's `com.apple.Accessibility` defaults for the rest; Xcode's own launch arguments for the pseudolanguages and
  right-to-left) and runs `QAMatrixTests`. **Before a screen is photographed the app is asked what it sees** (`-noctisEnvironmentProbe`: text size, bold,
  reduce motion, reduce transparency, differentiate without color, button shapes, on/off labels, invert, contrast, color scheme, layout direction, locale,
  calendar, time zone, hour format, first weekday) and the run fails if it is not the configuration it claims to be. `environmentOK` is that check.
- **Produced** means a top screenshot (and, for a page that scrolls, every page down to the bottom) exists for the combination and the screen reached
  its ready state. **Viewed** means a contact sheet that contains the combination's top and bottom was looked at at a size where its text can be read
  (`scripts/qa_sheets.py`: 340 to 460 pt a phone, the screen's name and the configuration's above each); `qa/coverage/viewed.tsv` is the list of sheets that
  were, with what was seen. Zoomed crops were taken where the text on a sheet was too small. **Result** is the worst of what was seen: PASS, NOTE (something
  seen that is not a defect, recorded) or FINDING (a ledger row).
- **UNVERIFIED** is everything else: not produced (and why, in the report), or produced and not looked at. It is never counted as a pass.

### What a screenshot cannot show

- **Reduce Motion** is a behavior: a still image of a screen with it on is the same as with it off. The configuration is verified by the probe and the
  screens are produced and viewed (nothing may depend on motion to be understood); what the setting changes is held by `HomeMotionQATests` and the
  motion tests of each screen (R2).
- **Smart Invert** is applied by the display, outside the app's layers: the simulator's screenshots are not inverted. The probe says the app sees it; the
  images prove only that nothing is lost when the app is told. Whether images and the Recovery ring are exempted correctly (`accessibilityIgnoresInvertColors`)
  needs a device or the simulator's screen recording, and is UNVERIFIED here.
- **Accented, Double-Length and Bounded String** change only what is looked up in a bundle's strings. A literal that is not looked up (an interpolated
  sentence) is not changed, which is itself what the run shows: `Localizations/needs-translation.csv` lists those.

### Rules for reading the Result column

- **The latest look wins.** A combination looked at again after a change (a later build) takes the result of the later look, page by page; a later PASS
  replaces an earlier FINDING, and a FINDING whose fix is "in source, UNVERIFIED as an image" stays a FINDING until a new image shows it fixed.
- **Recovered.** The matrix runs wrote their screenshots, manifests and contact sheets under `/tmp`, and the system's `/tmp` cleanup deleted them during an
  idle week, after they had been viewed (QA-114). What survives is `viewed.tsv`, the record of what each sheet showed. `scripts/qa_recover_sheets.py` rebuilds
  which combinations each sheet covered from the first words of its note (device, screen, configurations), into `recovered.tsv` and
  `recovered_produced.tsv`; a note that does not name its columns is left out and its combinations stay UNVERIFIED. A combination counted this way is
  marked **(recovered: image deleted)**: it was produced and looked at, but its image can no longer be looked at again. Everything re-photographed after
  that (the fixed build, from 2026-10-18) has its images in `~/qa-work/qamx` and is not marked.
- **Build.** A look before the fixes of QA part 2 saw the build of 2026-10-11; looks marked "FIXED BUILD" in their notes saw the build with the fixes of
  2026-10-12 (before the last edits, listed in the ledger as "fixed in source, UNVERIFIED as an image"); looks recorded from 2026-10-18 saw the final build.

| reviews.monthly.cover | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro and SE story cover (monthly), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: FINDINGS: the pinned Back and Next have no band behind them and the text runs under them at rest at AX5 ('were recorded' under Next, 'SEPTEMBER 2026' under Close in the bottom page at AX5), and on the SE at AX5 Next sits on 'average a night' and '+6 min' (fixed: the room under a page scales with the text and a band in the page's colour sits behind the pinned rows with a fade); the figures and lines scale and wrap whole |
| reviews.monthly.insights | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro insights page (monthly), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: three rows wrap to three to five lines each; FINDINGS: at AX3 and AX5 the pinned Back and Next sit over the third row at rest ('Back' over 'Behind your sleep…', 'Next' over the row) and in the bottom page at AX5 the first row scrolls under the progress segments and the Close button ('Later bedtime than usual' under the 2:00 clock) (fixed: a band in the page's colour behind the pinned rows, and the room under the page scales) |
| reviews.monthly.locked | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro locked month, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the card, its Premium pill and See Premium wrap and grow whole; FINDING: at AX5 'See Premium' sits under Done at rest and in the bottom page 'YOUR MONTH' is covered by the Close button ('OUR MONTH') (fixed as the other story pages) |
| reviews.monthly.majorChanges | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro major changes (monthly), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: change cards wrap to two and four lines whole; FINDING: at AX3 and AX5 the second card runs under Back and Next at rest, and in the AX5 bottom page the title's first line is struck through by the progress segments and the Close button (fixed) |
| reviews.monthly.records | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro records (monthly), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the two record cards grow and wrap whole; FINDING: at AX5 Back and Done sit over the second card at rest and 'This month's records' is struck through by the progress segments in the bottom page (fixed) |
| reviews.monthly.recoveryPattern | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro and SE recovery pattern (monthly), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the chart card grows to the page and the text wraps whole; FINDING: at AX3 and AX5 Back and Next sit over the chart and the first sentence at rest ('Back' over 'Highest 97', 'Next' over 'on Tue'), and in the bottom page the chart's '100' and '85' run under the progress segments and the Close button ('00', 'X5') and the title is struck through (fixed: a band behind the chrome, scaled inset) |
| reviews.monthly.sleepPattern | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro and SE sleep pattern (monthly), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the 30-bar chart scales with its card; FINDINGS: at AX3 and AX5 Back and Next sit over the chart and its legend at rest, and in the bottom page the first row of text and the legend ('8h need', '7h 41m average') are struck through by the progress segments and the Close button; the chart's legend wraps to two lines each ('8h / need', '7h 41m / average') (fixed: band behind the chrome, scaled inset) |
| reviews.monthly.timing | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro and SE timing (monthly), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the title wraps to three lines; the bedtime and wake-time chart's labels grow and the dots shrink to a strip at AX3 and AX5; FINDINGS: Back and Next sit over the legend and the first sentence at rest, and in the bottom page the title, legend ('Bedtime') and text are struck through by the progress segments and the Close button; on the SE at A-light the last paragraph runs under Back and Next ('n bedtime was 11:04 PM', 'ke time 6:59 AM' behind Back/Next: QA-070) (all fixed: band behind the chrome) |
| reviews.weekly.changes | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro weekly changes, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: biggest improvement and biggest change cards wrap whole; FINDING: Back and Next sit over the second card at AX3 and AX5 at rest, and in the bottom page the title and 'WHAT CHANGED' are struck through by the progress segments and the Close button (fixed) |
| reviews.weekly.consistency | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-chart: 17 Pro and SE weekly consistency, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: FINDINGS: (1) the chart's tick labels overprint at AX3 and AX5 ('12:00 AM4:00 AM8:00 AM' in a jumble) and the day letters in the 16 pt column are cut ('M' to 'N', 'W' to 'V') (fixed: chart ticks capped at xxLarge); (2) Back and Next over the chart and sentences at rest, and the legend and first sentences struck through by the progress segments and Close in the bottom pages (fixed: band behind the chrome); on the SE at A-light the last sentence runs under Back and Next (QA-070) |
| reviews.weekly.cover | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro and SE weekly cover and the duplicate insight page, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: figures and lines wrap whole; FINDING: at AX3 and AX5 Next sits over '7 of 7 nights were recorded' (and over 'the week before' on the SE at AX5) at rest, and 'OCT 4 - 10' is struck through by the progress segments and the Close button ('CT') in the bottom pages (fixed: band behind the chrome, scaled inset) |
| reviews.weekly.focus | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro and SE weekly focus, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the purple card and 'Ask Coach about this' grow whole; FINDING: at AX3 and AX5 the pinned Back and Done sit over the card's last lines and over 'Ask Coach about this' at rest on the SE, and the card's title is struck through by the progress segments and Close in the AX5 bottom pages (fixed) |
| reviews.weekly.insight | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-controls: 17 Pro and SE weekly cover and the duplicate insight page, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: figures and lines wrap whole; FINDING: at AX3 and AX5 Next sits over '7 of 7 nights were recorded' (and over 'the week before' on the SE at AX5) at rest, and 'OCT 4 - 10' is struck through by the progress segments and the Close button ('CT') in the bottom pages (fixed: band behind the chrome, scaled inset) |
| reviews.weekly.recovery | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-chart: 17 Pro and SE weekly recovery, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the line chart scales with the card and its ticks read ('100', '85', '70'); FINDINGS: Back and Next sit over the chart and the first sentence at rest at AX3 and AX5 (chart '70' and 'Highest 90' under them), and in the bottom pages the chart's ticks run under the progress segments and the Close button ('00', 'X5') and 'Rising' is struck through (fixed: band behind the chrome, scaled inset) |
| reviews.weekly.sleep | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-story-chart: 17 Pro and SE weekly sleep, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the seven stacked bars and the weekday letters scale ('S M T W T F S' readable at AX5), '8h need' legend whole; FINDINGS: Back and Next sit over the chart and the legend at rest at AX3 and AX5 (the first sentence 'On average a night was' under them), and in the bottom pages the title and the chart's top are struck through by the progress segments and Close (fixed: band behind the chrome, scaled inset) |
| settings.about | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-ax-rows: 17 Pro and SE About, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: FINDING: every row breaks its title inside the word at AX3 and AX5 ('Versi / on', 'Recov / ery', 'Sleep / qualit / y', 'Bedti / me', 'Reco / very') and the values wrap to two or three lines ('0.1.0 / (1)', 'recover / y-v3') because the tile, title and value share one row (fixed: the value goes under a one-line title that scales down to fit its word, no tile, at accessibility sizes); the notes and the medical-device card wrap whole |
| settings.appearance | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | PASS |
| settings.apple-watch | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-ax-rows: 17 Pro Apple Watch settings, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the connected card, the update button ('Send update now' to two lines with the watch glyph at AX5) and the four 'on your watch' rows wrap whole; FINDING: at AX5 'Send update now' wraps into 'Send update / now' under the tab bar at rest and the watch glyph sits on its own; the rows' icon tiles take a third of the width at AX3 and AX5 ('Your recovery score and what went into it' in a 190pt column) (fixed in NoctisRow: no tile at accessibility sizes) |
| settings.data-management | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-ax-rows: 17 Pro Data management, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: FINDING: every kind's title and its action break inside words at AX3 and AX5 ('Jour / nal', 'Delet / e', 'Insight / s history', 'Nothin / g stored') because the tile, title, detail and the Delete action share one row (fixed: no tile and a one-line title scaled to its word at accessibility sizes); 'Delete all NOCTIS data' wraps to two lines whole at AX3 and AX5; the export card and notes wrap whole |
| settings.health-data | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-ax-rows: 17 Pro Health data, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the five signals rows grow and wrap whole, the Open Health button and How access works scale; FINDING: the section label wraps to two lines and the rows' checks and tiles take the left column (fixed: no tile at accessibility sizes); nothing broken inside a word |
| settings.health-data.unauthorized | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-ax-rows: 17 Pro and SE Health data (unauthorized), A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the card, Connect Apple Health, five rows and Open Health wrap whole; FINDING: at AX5 on the 17 Pro 'Respirator / y Rate' breaks inside its word (tile and status leave the title 190pt) (fixed: no tile and a one-line title scaled to its word) |
| settings.help | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-ax-rows: 17 Pro and SE Help, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: the eight collapsed questions wrap to three or four lines each whole with the chevron at the trailing edge; nothing is cut or broken inside a word; at AX5 the first screen shows two questions under the tab bar and the page scrolls: acceptable, no finding |
| settings.notifications | A-light | QA17Pro | yes | yes | FINDING QA-ax-rows: 17 Pro and SE Notifications, A-light against B-AX3 and B-AX5, top and bottom: FINDINGS: the 'Time' row breaks its title into 'T / i / m / e' at AX3 (and 'Ti / m / e' on the Pro) because the time picker takes most of the row; 'Day' and 'Sunday' break ('Sund / ay', 'Sunda / y') at AX5; 'Morning summary' and 'Weekly review' rows' titles and the 'Says ...' lines are cut by the toggle column; the time picker's text is cut off at AX5 (the pill is visible and the label '9:00 AM' whole) (fixed in NoctisRow: no tile and a title that wraps at spaces and scales to its word at accessibility sizes); the banner wraps whole |

| coach.answer | D-DoubleLength | QASE | yes | yes | FINDING QA-coach-pseudo-route: SE and 17 Pro, D-RTL against D-DoubleLength and D-Bounded: D-RTL shows the real answer (70, 'Your recovery is 70 today, 17 points below your usual', What's behind this rows mirrored with the icon on the right, What changed / What to notice, confidence pill, Open Recovery, feedback buttons, follow-up chips scrolling sideways); FINDING: under D-DoubleLength and D-Bounded the conversation does not reach the answer (the title shows the generic 'I can help with questions about your sleep and recovery' list or 'Coach couldn't answer that just now'): the question text goes through the router's patterns, which are in the same catalog and pseudolocalized too, so they stop matching; a translation whose text and patterns disagree would do the same. Layout of the scripted states was viewed (fine); the answered state under those two is UNVERIFIED. SE bottom of coach.answer under D-RTL and the others is the same page (it did not move: the pinned chips) (recovered: image deleted) |
